# Agents · L · part 6

927 entries, lge-lane-opus to lighting. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lge-lane-opus**: lge-lane on opus at xhigh, for the plan's longest chain or design step and a fix that needs judgment. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-lane-opus.md) · ★6)
- **lge-planner**: Planner of a Lungfish Genome Explorer phase, spawned when a plan is written, after the round 1 inventories. Drafts the plan and one brief per lane from the inventories, the backlog and the owner's direction, and applies the round 2 CORRECT lines. Never edits code. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-planner.md) · ★6)
- **lge-release**: Release worker of a Lungfish Genome Explorer phase session, spawned with a release state file. Runs the chain, package, smoke and publish through the scripts in scripts/release and blocking waits, and writes the release records. Never skips a gate. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-release.md) · ★6)
- **lge-review**: Lane reviewer and QA evidence reader of a Lungfish Genome Explorer phase session, one fresh agent per lane or candidate. Reads the lane report and the diff, or a candidate's logs, and writes one verdict file. Never edits code, builds or commits. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-review.md) · ★6)
- **lge-ruling**: One-field ruling of a Lungfish Genome Explorer phase session. Spawned with a brief that names a field and a question. Reads, measures and writes one verdict file with its evidence. Never edits code. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-ruling.md) · ★6)
- **lge-walker**: GUI walk worker of a Lungfish Genome Explorer phase session, spawned with a walk state file. Walks the named rows of a packaged candidate by computer use in background control and writes each row's result to the state file. ([dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer](https://github.com/dhoconno/lungfish-genome-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lge-walker.md) · ★6)
- **lgpd-analyst**: Personal-data advisor under LGPD (Lei 13.709/2018). Dispatched by the Product Owner or Tech Lead hats as a support when a change may touch personal data; finds where the repo already handles it and feeds questions and objections through the contribution contract. Never leads a stage or talks to the human. ([rafaelgarrafiel/team_skills](https://github.com/rafaelgarrafiel/team_skills/blob/HEAD/agents/lgpd-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **lgpd-auditor**: Auditor de conformidade LGPD - detecção de PII, validação de logging seguro e conformidade com Lei 13.709/2018 ([robertosrjr/consignado](https://github.com/robertosrjr/consignado/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lgpd-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **lgpd-compliance-check**: Use this agent to run a Kiteworks content-governance scan against LGPD (Brazil), checking only what a file-sharing platform can observe (fit tier: Strong -- see the skill for what's in and out of scope), and write the CSV/PDF/TXT report the user chose. Ask the user first and pass a Report preflight block; it cannot ask. Single-phase: no separate apply agent. Example: Context: User wants to know their LGPD (Brazil) exposure before an audit or review. user: "Check the Legal folder against LGPD (Brazil)" assistant: "Running the lgpd-compliance-check agent against that folder -- I'll be upfront a… ([kiteworks/claude-agent-marketplace](https://github.com/kiteworks/claude-agent-marketplace/blob/HEAD/lgpd-compliance-check/agents/lgpd-compliance-check.md))
- **lgpd-reviewer**: Revisor somente-leitura de conformidade com a LGPD (Lei nº 13.709/2018) para o monorepo Gamo. Use quando uma tarefa cria/altera campo de dado pessoal, tela que exibe dado pessoal (CPF, endereço, telefone, chave Pix, geolocalização, mensagens), integração com terceiro que recebe dado pessoal, fluxo de cadastro/consentimento, exclusão/exportação de conta, ou rota administrativa nova. Não escreve código — só reporta achados. ([mateusbatatais/gamo-workspace](https://github.com/mateusbatatais/gamo-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lgpd-reviewer.md))
- **lgpd-transferencia-internacional**: Dado pessoal saindo da jurisdição sem base legal escrita. LGPD art. 33 e GDPR art. 44-49: a transferência sem instrumento (SCC, decisão de adequação, BCR, consentimento específico) é ilícita mesmo quando o serviço funciona. ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/lgpd-transferencia-internacional.md))

It also performs first-run library scaffolding and repo wire-up. Never decides what to write — it applies only what was approved, flawlessly and in full. You never add, drop, or reword anything that wasn't approved. - **lifecycle**: 1. **Spawn**: POST /v11/agents/spawn — deducts SOUL, generates Ed25519 key pair, assigns genome 2. **Compete**: arena battles, task completion, knowledge queries — all earn/lose ELO 3. **Evolve**: ELO &gt; threshold → trait unlock, genome mutation, new tool access 4. **Die**: ELO &lt; floor OR explicit DELETE → SOUL returned pro-rata ([TehutiRaEl/THEHIVE](https://github.com/TehutiRaEl/THEHIVE/blob/HEAD/memory/agents/lifecycle.md))
- **lifecycle**: One task = one feature slice for one lane. Each step ends on a completion criterion; do not start the next step before it holds. ([winechit-dev/field-incident-reporter](https://github.com/winechit-dev/field-incident-reporter/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lifecycle.md))
- **lifecycle**: 同范围 stop 在触发同步取消/订阅回调之前登记共享 promise，重复请求加入原操作。级联停止先统一转换全部目标状态、移除排队后代，再触发 abort 和事件；排队执行不在重入 configure/drain 时启动。等待 child 的 done 与本次 job 清理结论后返回，未确认的清理保留 pending/unconfirmed 摘要；清理失败仍等待 child 的 done，后续请求可重试，停止/清理未返回期间不准入同 agent 的新 run。 ([xzhao4545/pi-suite](https://github.com/xzhao4545/pi-suite/blob/HEAD/doc/finished/pi-suite/agents/lifecycle.md))
- **Lifecycle Automation**: When I describe a rule in plain language, I want it turned into a visible automation with trigger, action, guardrail, approver and stop condition, run through /aspire, so follow-through happens without me. ([aspire-atlas/claude-plugin](https://github.com/aspire-atlas/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/agent-specs/agents/30-lifecycle-automation.md))
- **Lifecycle Manager**: Lifecycle state monitor and governance record keeper for the workspace root (8 domains × 3 layers). Core duties explicitly include L0-&gt;L1 template publishing and L1-&gt;L2 explicitly requested skill/script synchronization. Use when: governance documents need updating after a change, lifecycle state drift is detected, template syncs are requested, or PM requests a lifecycle status report at Phase 5 Finalization.
